begin

      begin
      neg_out<=neg_cnt;
      end

function func_code
begin
input [8:0]func_din
    case(func_din)
        128:
        begin
        func_code=7;
        end

        64:
        begin
        func_code=6;
        end

        32:
        begin
        func_code=5;
        end

        16:
        begin
        func_code=4;
        end

        8:
        begin
        func_code=3;
        end

        4:
        begin
        func_code=2;
        end

        2:
        begin
        func_code=1;
        end

        0:
        begin
        func_code=0;
        end

        default:
        begin
        func_code=0;
        end

        endcase


end
endfunction

begin
  if(!rst_n)
    begin
    data_b<=0;
    data_b1<=0;
    end

      begin
      data_b<=data_b1;
      end
    else if(valid_a==1)
        begin
        data_b1<=data_a,data_b1[5:1];
        end
end

function [2,0] func_code
begin
input [7:0]func_din
    case(func_din)
        128:
        begin
        func_code=7;
        end

        64:
        begin
        func_code=6;
        end

        32:
        begin
        func_code=5;
        end

        16:
        begin
        func_code=4;
        end

        8:
        begin
        func_code=3;
        end

        4:
        begin
        func_code=2;
        end

        2:
        begin
        func_code=1;
        end

        0:
        begin
        func_code=0;
        end

        default:
        begin
        func_code=0;
        end

        endcase


end
endfunction
